# Raw Cod
Aliases: Raw Fish, Raw cod
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Raw Cod Collection, Collections, Fishing
Raw Cod is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Raw Cod.
[Raw Cod]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Minion XP (The amount of xp a player receives when taking this material from a minion inventory.): +0.5 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
Uses: [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]; [[Fish Hat]]; Fishing Baits ([[Bait]]); [[Fishing Minion]] upgrades
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Beginner Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Medium Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Large Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 6 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RAW_FISH
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Raw Cod is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Fishing]] [[Collections|Collection]] item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|20|[[Fish Hat]] Recipe; [[Minnow Bait]] Recipe|+4|
|II|50|[[Fishing Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|III|100|Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|250|[[Pond Island]] Recipe|+4|
|V|500|Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|VI|1,000|[[Enchanted Raw Cod]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|2,500|Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|VIII|15,000|[[Enchanted Cooked Cod]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|30,000||+4|
|X|45,000||+4|
|XI|60,000||+4|
## Obtaining
### Fishing
Raw Cod can be fished from any water source block available. It is the most common item in the [[Fishing]] [[Collections|Collection]]. When caught, Raw Cod grants +25 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]].
### Minions
Raw Cod can be obtained using the [[Fishing Minion]]. This method is best for low-level fishing or for overnight fishing experience.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv59]|[[Catfish]]|20|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv119]|[[Water Hydra]]|20|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv75]|[[Guardian Defender]]|15|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv49]|Bloated Mithril Grubber ([[Mithril Grubber]])|12-24|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv74]|[[Sea Leech]]|10|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv41]|Large Mithril Grubber ([[Mithril Grubber]])|8-12|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv33]|Medium Mithril Grubber ([[Mithril Grubber]])|4-8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv29]|[[Mithril Grubber]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv36]|[[Sea Archer]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Sea Walker]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Timil]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Shop Purchase
Up to 640 Raw Cod can be bought from the [[Fishing Merchant]] for 10 [[Coins]] per day.

16x Raw Mutton is a potential reward from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s buried treasure.
### Merchants
Raw Mutton can be obtained from the [[Husbandry Merchant]] located in [[The Barn]].

|[[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Raw Mutton V (5)|[[Raw Mutton]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Raw Mutton|Recipe: Raw Mutton x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Sheep Pet]]|Raw Mutton III (3)|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] [[Raw Mutton]] 256x Raw Mutton|Recipe: Raw Mutton x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [[Sheep Pet]]|
|Beginner Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]])|Raw Mutton II (2)|1x [[Leather]] [[Raw Mutton]] 8x Raw Mutton|Recipe: Raw Mutton ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Husbandry Sack]]|
### [[Potions|Brewing]] Ingredient
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Mana I Potion ([[Mana Potion]])|Raw Mutton IV (4)|[[Raw Mutton]] 1x Raw Mutton 3x [[Awkward Potion]]|Recipe: Raw Mutton → [[Mana Potion]], [[Mana Potion]], [[Mana Potion]]|
### [[Kat]] Pet Sitting
512x Raw Mutton can be used to upgrade a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Sheep Pet]] to [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] at [[Kat]].
## Trivia
- [[Polymorph Wand]] is an unreleased sword that was unlocked at Raw Mutton IX (9).

## Obtaining
### Fishing
Raw Salmon can be fished from any water source block available. It is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item in the [[Fishing]] collection.
### Minions
Raw Salmon can be obtained using the [[Fishing Minion]]. This method is best for low level fishing or for overnight fishing experience.

# Redstone Dust

Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Pages with UIs, Mob Drops, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Redstone Dust Collection, Collections, Brewing ingredients
Redstone Dust is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Redstone Dust.
[Redstone Dust]
Type: Collection item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Mad Redstone Engineer]]
Buy: 4 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REDSTONE
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient Collection Item; COMMON
Redstone Dust is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Collections|Collection]] item in the [[Mining]] category. It can be obtained by mining [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] in the [[Pigmen's Den]], [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Collection
Collecting [[Redstone Dust]] Redstone Dust increases the player's Redstone Dust [[Collections|Collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Redstone Dust.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|[[Redstone Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|250|[[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|III|750|[[Efficiency]] Exp Discount (-25%); +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|IV|1,500|[[Weather Wand]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Personal Compactor 4000]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|VII|25,000|[[Enchanted Redstone Block]] Recipe; [[Personal Compactor 5000]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|VIII|100,000|[[Personal Compactor 6000]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
|IX|250,000|[[Personal Compactor 7000]] Recipe; +6 [[Accessory Bag]] Slots|+4|
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Redstone Ore spawns naturally in the [[Pigmen's Den]], where it can be mined and collected by the player. Normally, it drops a number of Redstone pieces and grants +7 [[Mining]] XP. However, mining it this way attracts [[Redstone Pigman|Redstone Pigmen]] to attack to player, making it inconvenient to collect for early game player. The player can collect the [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] directly with a [[Silk Touch]] pickaxe, then bring it back to their island to mine afterwards. Although this does not grant mining XP, [[Redstone Pigman]] do not appear to be aggravated at players who mine [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] in this way, and it also saves valuable inventory space. [[Redstone Pigman]] will only attack the player if they have at least 1 stack of Redstone Dust in their inventory; mining with a [[Mining Sack]] with more than 64 Redstone Dust will not attract the attention of [[Redstone Pigman|Redstone Pigmen]]. Redstone can also be safely collected via mining the Redstone, then crafting it into a [[Block of Redstone]] before having more than 1 stack.
In the [[Dwarven Mines]], small amounts of [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] may be mined from decorative parts of the map, such as the carts in [[Rampart's Quarry]], though a more efficient way of getting Redstone are the several veins of [[Ores|Pure Redstone]], which are located in the [[Royal Mines]].
In the [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] may be found throughout the map. [[Ores|Pure Redstone]] can be found in the [[Mines of Divan]], though less than in the [[Royal Mines]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv8]|[[Redstone Pigman (Stranded)]]|12|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv8]|[[Redstone Pigman (Stranded)]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv8]|[[Redstone Pigman]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv144]|[[Wither Spectre]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|20%|
### Purchasing
[[Redstone Dust]] 640 (10 stacks) Redstone Dust is able to be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] on the bottom floor of the [[Builder]]'s house each day for 4 [[Coins]] each.
### Minions
Redstone can be obtained from [[Redstone Minion|Redstone Minions]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Block of Redstone]]|Recipe: [[Block of Redstone]] → Redstone Dust x9|
## Usage
### Redstone Meter
The Redstone meter
Redstone functions similar to how it does within vanilla Minecraft, although certain items, such as Pistons and Dispensers, are inoperable, and there is a limit to the amount of active Redstone a player can have on their [[Private Island]]. This limit is displayed on the side scoreboard, but only when any amount of Redstone is being used, and it disappears when none is being used. Redstone is more likely to be used as a crafting ingredient due to a lack of operable inputs and outputs.
### Selling
Redstone Dust can be sold to any [[Shops|Merchant]] for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] a piece.
### Brewing
Redstone Dust can be used in [[Potions|Brewing]] to boost potion duration to 8 minutes, (splash potions halve the duration, unless brewed with enchanted gunpowder) with your [[Alchemy]] level boosting it further. [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] provides a boost of up to 16 minutes, and the [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] will bring it up to 40 minutes.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Magnetic Talisman]]|Emerald III (3) ([[Emerald]])|[[Redstone Dust]] 32x Redstone Dust 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Emerald]]|Recipe: [[Emerald]] x32 ×4 + Redstone Dust x32 → [[Magnetic Talisman]]|
|[[Magical Mushroom Soup]]|Mushroom IV (4) ([[Mushroom]])|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] [[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 1x [[Glowstone Dust]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Glowstone Dust]] + [[Mushroom]] ×2 + Redstone Dust + [[Bowl]] → [[Magical Mushroom Soup]]|
|Redstone Minion I ([[Redstone Minion]])|Redstone Dust I (1)|1x [[Wooden Pickaxe]] [[Redstone Dust]] 128x Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust x16 ×8 + [[Wooden Pickaxe]] → [[Redstone Minion]]|
|Redstone Minion II ([[Redstone Minion]])|Redstone Dust I (1)|1x Redstone Minion I ([[Redstone Minion]]) [[Redstone Dust]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust x32 ×8 + [[Redstone Minion]] → [[Redstone Minion]]|
|Redstone Minion III ([[Redstone Minion]])|Redstone Dust I (1)|1x Redstone Minion II ([[Redstone Minion]]) [[Redstone Dust]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust x64 ×8 + [[Redstone Minion]] → [[Redstone Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Redstone Dust IV (4)|[[Redstone Dust]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|
|[[Repeater]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 2x [[Redstone Torch]] 3x [[Stone]]|Recipe: [[Redstone Torch]] ×2 + Redstone Dust + [[Stone]] ×3 → [[Repeater]]|
|[[Redstone Torch]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: Redstone Dust + [[Stick]] → [[Redstone Torch]]|
|[[Noteblock]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 8x [[Planks]]|Recipe: [[Planks]] ×8 + Redstone Dust → [[Noteblock]]|
|[[Compass]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 4x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×4 + Redstone Dust → [[Compass]]|
|[[Block of Redstone]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 9x Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust ×9 → [[Block of Redstone]]|
|[[Powered Rail]]|None|1x [[Stick]] [[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 6x [[Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] ×6 + [[Stick]] + Redstone Dust → [[Powered Rail]] x6|
|[[Detector Rail]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 1x [[Stone Pressure Plate]] 6x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×6 + [[Stone Pressure Plate]] + Redstone Dust → [[Detector Rail]] x6|
|[[Dropper]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 7x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×7 + Redstone Dust → [[Dropper]]|

|[[Piston]]|None|[[Redstone Dust]] 1x Redstone Dust 1x [[Iron Ingot]] 3x [[Planks]] 4x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Planks]] ×3 + [[Cobblestone]] ×4 + [[Iron Ingot]] + Redstone Dust → [[Piston]]|
|[[Redstone Lamp]]|None|1x [[Glowstone]] [[Redstone Dust]] 4x Redstone Dust|Recipe: Redstone Dust ×4 + [[Glowstone]] → [[Redstone Lamp]]|
|[[Hotspot Radar]]|None|1x [[Broken Radar]] 2x [[Bronze Bowl]] [[Redstone Dust]] 2x Redstone Dust 3x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Bronze Bowl]] ×2 + Redstone Dust ×2 + [[Broken Radar]] + [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 → [[Hotspot Radar]]|
|[[Red Sweater]]|Lotus II (2) ([[Lotus]])|64x [[Lotus]] [[Redstone Dust]] 128x Redstone Dust|Recipe: [[Lotus]] x16 ×4 + Redstone Dust x32 ×4 → [[Red Sweater]]|

## Usage
### Private Island
Redstone Lamps can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. When activated from a [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] signal, it will turn on, but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often this can be done. Redstone is disabled on [[The Garden]].
## Game Messages

|-|-|
|Please note that redstone is currently disabled on this island!|Placing a Redstone Lamp in [[The Garden]]|

Saddle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants +30 [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Leather VIII (8) ([[Leather]])|8x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 24x [[Enchanted Leather]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Leather]] x8 ×3 + [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x8 → Saddle|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Saddle will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +30 [[Speed]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Saddle for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- [[Crafting]] a Saddle for the first time grants the [[Achievements|"Saddle Up" Achievement]].

Sand is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mining]] [[Collections|Collection]] block.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Sand Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|+250 [[Mining]]Experience|+4|
|III|250|[[Soul Sand]] Trade|+4|
|IV|500|Desert Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) Recipe|+4|
|V|1,000|[[Enchanted Sand]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|2,500|[[Burning Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|5,000|[[Hard Glass]] Recipe|+4|
Collecting Sand grants +3 [[Mining|Mining XP]].
## Obtaining
Sand can be mined in the [[Mushroom Desert]]. It is also possible to collect it on the [[Private Island]] through [[Sand Minion|Sand Minions]], or by placing [[Desert Island|Desert Islands]] or [[Pond Island|Pond Islands]], and then digging the Sand.
### Shop Purchases
Sand can be bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] or [[Windmill Operator]] for 4 [[Coins]], or from the [[Builder]] for 2 [[Coins]] each.
### Treasure Hunter
[[Sand]] 16 Sand is a potential reward from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s buried treasure.
### Tick Thresholds
Show/Hide Tick Thresholds
Mining Time (in Ticks): Necessary and sufficient conditions while mining…
Mining Time (in Ticks): [[Sand]] Sand
15: Default
14: [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
13: Haste I ([[Haste Potion]])
12: [More Info Needed]
11: Haste II ([[Haste Potion]])
10: Haste III ([[Haste Potion]])
9: Haste IV ([[Haste Potion]])
8: [[Wooden Shovel]]
7: [[Wooden Shovel]]; Haste I ([[Haste Potion]])
6: [[Wooden Shovel]]; Haste II ([[Haste Potion]])
5: [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste II ([[Haste Potion]]); 1 M. Penalty
4 (Softcap): [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste IV ([[Haste Potion]]); 1 M. Penalty
3: [[Iron Shovel]]
2: [[Golden Shovel]]
1 (Instamine): Efficiency II ([[Efficiency]]) [[Golden Shovel]]
1 (Instamine): [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste II ([[Haste Potion]])
## Usage
Sand must be placed around a [[Cactus Minion]] for it to work and can be placed around a [[Sugar Cane Minion]] with [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].
Sand can be smelted in a [[Furnace]] or mined with a tool with [[Smelting Touch]] to obtain [[Glass]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pond Island]]|Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])|3x [[Water Bucket]] [[Sand]] 15x Sand 60x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: [[Water Bucket]] ×3 + Sand x5 ×3 + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x20 ×3 → [[Pond Island]]|
|Sand Minion I ([[Sand Minion]])|Sand I (1)|1x [[Wooden Shovel]] [[Sand]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Sand|Recipe: Sand x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Shovel]] → [[Sand Minion]]|
|Sand Minion II ([[Sand Minion]])|Sand I (1)|1x Sand Minion I ([[Sand Minion]]) [[Sand]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Sand|Recipe: Sand x20 ×8 + [[Sand Minion]] → [[Sand Minion]]|
|Sand Minion III ([[Sand Minion]])|Sand I (1)|1x Sand Minion II ([[Sand Minion]]) [[Sand]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Sand|Recipe: Sand x40 ×8 + [[Sand Minion]] → [[Sand Minion]]|
|Sand Minion IV ([[Sand Minion]])|Sand I (1)|1x Sand Minion III ([[Sand Minion]]) [[Sand]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Sand|Recipe: Sand x64 ×8 + [[Sand Minion]] → [[Sand Minion]]|
|[[Desert Island]]|Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]])|5x [[Sugar Cane]] 10x [[Cactus]] [[Sand]] 30x Sand|Recipe: [[Cactus]] x5 ×2 + [[Sugar Cane]] x5 + Sand x5 ×6 → [[Desert Island]]|
|Desert Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Sand IV (4)|2x [[Stick]] [[Sand]] 64x Sand|Recipe: Sand x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|[[Enchanted Sand]]|Sand V (5)|[[Sand]] 160x Sand|Recipe: Sand x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Sand]]|
|[[TNT]]|None|[[Sand]] 4x Sand 5x [[Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Gunpowder]] ×5 + Sand ×4 → [[TNT]]|
|[[Sandstone]]|None|[[Sand]] 4x Sand|Recipe: Sand ×4 → [[Sandstone]]|
## Tips
- A good early-game tactic for getting [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]] to access the [[Dwarven Mines]] is to make an [[Efficiency|Efficiency II]] [[Golden Shovel]] and mine Sand in the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Trivia
- Until the 0.11.4 update, players weren't able to mine Sand on any public island. Instead, a [[Desert Island]] was needed to obtain Sand.

# Saplings
Aliases: Acacia Sapling, Birch Sapling, Dark Oak Sapling, Jungle Sapling, Oak Sapling, Sapling, Spruce Sapling, Types of Sapling
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items
Saplings is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Saplings.
[Saplings]
Type: Vanilla item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: Decayed leaves
Uses: Planting trees [[Trades]] material
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]

Buy: 2 [[Coins]]
Sell: 0.25 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SAPLING (Oak Sapling); SAPLING:1 (Spruce Sapling); SAPLING:2 (Birch Sapling); SAPLING:3 (Jungle Sapling); SAPLING:4 (Acacia Sapling); SAPLING:5 (Dark Oak Sapling)
Tooltip: COMMON
Saplings are [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla items.
There are 6 types of saplings, one for each type of tree in Minecraft. They can be obtained by destroying leaves normally (without using shears or a tool with the [[Silk Touch]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]]), or purchased from the [[Builder]]. Other than growing a tree on a [[Private Island]], their only use is to [[Trades|Trade]] for the corresponding type of [[Leaves]].
## Obtaining
Trees grow naturally in the [[Forest]] and [[The Park]], where you can break logs and the decaying leaves will drop saplings. It is also possible to plant saplings on your private island, which grow normally and will drop additional saplings once chopped down.
They can also be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 2 [[Coins]] each.
|Icon|Type|Trade Unlock|Tree Location|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Saplings]]|Oak|Oak Log II (2) ([[Oak Log]])|[[Forest]]|
|[[Saplings]]|Spruce|Spruce Log II (2) ([[Spruce Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Saplings]]|Birch|Birch Log II (2) ([[Birch Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Saplings]]|Dark Oak|Dark Oak Log II (2) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Saplings]]|Acacia|Acacia Log II (2) ([[Acacia Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Saplings]]|Jungle|Jungle Log II (2) ([[Jungle Log]])|[[The Park]]|
## Usage
Saplings can be used to grow trees on your [[Private Island]], or unlock trades on all second tier of the [[Foraging]] collections.
|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|Oak Log II (2) ([[Oak Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Birch Log II (2) ([[Birch Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Spruce Log II (2) ([[Spruce Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Dark Oak Log II (2) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Acacia Log II (2) ([[Acacia Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Jungle Log II (2) ([[Jungle Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
## Trivia
- Saplings are tied with [[Stick]] to have the lowest NPC sell price of all items, 0.25 [[Coins]].

# Scaffolding
Categories: Vanilla objects, More info needed, Fig Log Collection, Tools
Scaffolding is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Scaffolding.
[Scaffolding]
Scaffolding is a category of tools mainly used for [[Foraging]]. They can be placed, allowing players to climb up and reach hard-to-reach spots on certain trees.
## List of Scaffolding
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|Height|Radius|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tiny Scaffolding]]|[[Tiny Scaffolding]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Fig Log I (1) ([[Fig Log]])|2 → 4|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|
|[[Medium Scaffolding]]|[[Medium Scaffolding]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Fig Log III (3) ([[Fig Log]])|3 → 9|[More Info Needed]|
|[[Large Scaffolding]]|[[Large Scaffolding]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Fig Log V (5) ([[Fig Log]])|4 → 16|8 blocks|
## Usage
Demonstration of the [[Large Scaffolding]]
Scaffolding, being a climbable semi-solid block, allow the player to climb up and down. They can only be used on [[Galatea]]: attempting to use Scaffolding outside of [[Galatea]] yields the following message.
You must be on Galatea in order to use this item!
All pieces of Scaffolding inherit the Climb! ability, allowing the player to place up to a certain limit: for example, the [[Tiny Scaffolding]] allows the player to place 2 Scaffolding blocks per interaction, up to 4 Scaffolding blocks placed. Regardless of where the player physically places a Scaffolding, it will appear beneath the player.
Being more than than a certain distance from any placed Scaffolding will cause it to break. Additionally, holding Scaffolding will replace the [[SkyBlock Menu]] with a [[Scaffolding]] Scaffolding block.
Scaffolding, however, has several limitations.
- Scaffolding cannot be placed horizontally.
- Scaffolding cannot be broken. In order to regain one's available Scaffolding, the player must leave its radius.
- Placing Scaffolding in an invalid position yields the following message: You can't place a scaffolding here!
- Placing Scaffolding in a location that is too low to accomodate all Scaffolding blocks yields the following message: There's not enough space above you to place the scaffolding!
- Using all of one's available Scaffolding and attempting to place more yields the following message: You can't use more scaffolding blocks.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Scaffolding.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Scaffolding.|
Linked from: [[Large Scaffolding]], [[Medium Scaffolding]], [[Player]], [[Tiny Scaffolding]]

Sacks: Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]); Small Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]); Medium Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]); Large Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 3 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SEEDS
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Seeds are a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Farming]] [[Collections|Collection]] item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Dirt]] Trade|+4|
|II|100|[[Clay Ball]] Trade|+4|
|III|250|[[Long Grass]] Trade; [[Enchanted Seeds]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Fern]] Trade|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Dead Bush]] Trade|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Double Tallgrass]] Trade|+4|
|VII|25,000|[[Box of Seeds]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Seeds can be collected at the [[Farm]], [[The Barn]], or [[The Garden]] by harvesting [[Wheat]]. They are affected by [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]].
Seeds can be generated by [[Wheat Minion|Wheat Minions]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Nether Wart]]|[[Stranded]] only (This content is only available on the Stranded game mode.)|1x [[Fermented Spider Eye]] [[Seeds]] 4x Seeds|Recipe: Seeds ×4 + [[Fermented Spider Eye]] → [[Nether Wart]]|
|[[Enchanted Seeds]]|Seeds III (3)|[[Seeds]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Seeds|Recipe: Seeds x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Seeds]]|
### Trades
|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|[[Seeds]] Seeds I (1)|Seeds x8 →|→ [[Dirt]] x2|
|[[Seeds]] Seeds II (2)|Seeds x12 →|→ [[Clay Ball]]|

# Sheep
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Sheep is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Sheep.
[Sheep]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Oasis]] [[Shepherd's Keep]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 1x White Wool ([[Wool]]) 1x [[Raw Mutton]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: FARMING_SHEEP_1
Sheep are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found in the [[Oasis]] and [[Shepherd's Keep]].
## Behavior
Sheep are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.
Sheep can be sheared to obtain [[Wool|White Wool]] and obtain +3 [[Farming|Farming XP]]. They can also be brought to [[Jake]] in the [[Desert Mountain]] using a [[Lead]] to unlock the [[Advanced Sheep Axe]] in his shop.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Raw Mutton]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|White Wool ([[Wool]])|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|The Farming Islands ([[Bestiary/List]])|Sheep|Beep beep I'm a sheep.|1|5|200|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|40|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|60|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|100|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|200|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Trivia
- Sheep's [[Bestiary]] description is a reference to the asdfmovie web series by TomSka and the Beep Beep I'm a Sheep song by LilDeuceDeuce.

## Behavior
Sheep are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.

# Shovels
Aliases: Shovel
Categories: Vanilla objects, Tools, Other Tools
Shovels are used in [[Mining]] to obtain certain blocks such as [[Gravel]] and [[Dirt]]. Shovels can also be used for crafting [[Gravel Minion]], [[Sand Minion]], [[Mycelium Minion]], and [[Red Sand Minion]].
## Non-Vanilla Shovels
|Icon|Name|Rarity|Ability|Source|Recipe|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Promising Shovel]]|[[Promising Shovel]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Gains a higher level of Enchantment (Up to Efficiency X ([[Efficiency]])) when breaking blocks. Has a chance to drop double flint, up to 10%.|Reward from The Flint Bros ([[Quests]]) quest||
|[[Flint Shovel]]|[[Flint Shovel]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Grants a 100% chance of receiving [[Flint]] from [[Gravel]]. This item can be also used as a minion upgrade to allow Gravel Minions ([[Gravel Minion]]) to always receive flint. Note that this flint will still count towards the Gravel collection.|Gravel II (2) ([[Gravel]])|Recipe: [[Flint]] x10 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Flint Shovel]]|
|[[Snow Shovel]]|[[Snow Shovel]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Item Ability: Snow Placer Right-Click a block to place a 3x3 snow layer. Cost: 10 [[Coins]]|[[Scoop]]||
## Vanilla Shovels
Shovels is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Shovels.
All vanilla Minecraft tools exist in SkyBlock. Since tools don't degrade on use in SkyBlock, golden tools are actually the best due to their speed. The wooden version is used in various [[Minions|Minion]] recipes.
|Icon|Type|Recipe|Rarity|Damage|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wooden Shovel]]|Wooden|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Planks]] + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|15|
|[[Golden Shovel]]|Golden|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|15|
|[[Stone Shovel]]|Stone|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|20|
|[[Iron Shovel]]|Iron|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|25|
|[[Diamond Shovel]]|Diamond|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Diamond]] + [[Stick]] ×2|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|30|
## Trivia
- Within the [[Blacksmith|Reforge Guide]], [[Daedalus' Notes]] — a reforge stone for [[Ancestral Spade]] and its upgrades — is located in its own category of Shovel Reforges, despite no Shovels being reforgeable.

[Non-item drops - [Lv1-12]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1-4|1-4|1-4|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5-18|5-18|5-18|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1-13|1-13|1-13|
## Behavior
Slimes jump towards the player and attempt to hit them, dealing [[Damage]]. When killed, they do not split into smaller Slimes.
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Private Island ([[Bestiary/List]])|Slime ([[Slime (disambiguation)]])|World's loudest creatures.|2|5|50|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|10|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|15|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|25|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|50|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Tips
- Slimes can spawn in light, therefore it is recommended to make a lit up platform below Y=30 if specifically grinding Slimes, so that other mobs can't spawn.

# Snowball
Aliases: Snow
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
Snowball is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Snowball.
[Snowball]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: Mining snow layers on Taiga island biome with Taiga Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) and Weather Stick ([[Weather Wand]]) Mining snow layers made from the [[Snow Shovel]] Fishing in [[Jerry Pond]] [[Snow Minion]]
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Snow Block]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Winter Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SNOW_BALL
Tooltip: COMMON
Snowball is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla item.
## Obtaining
Snowballs can be obtained from fishing in the [[Jerry Pond]], as well as from [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]]. You could also obtain them by using a [[Biome Stick|Taiga Biome Stick]], then using a [[Weather Wand]] to change the weather to rain, which would result in snow in the Taiga, which can then be harvested.
Another way to obtain snow is by buying [[Snow Shovel]] from [[Scoop]] located at the [[Mountain]] and using it to create snow layers in your island to collect.
## Usage
In the [[Jerry Pond]], if you pick up snowballs and you have [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]] then instead of picking them up, it gets filled by 1 for every snowball until it's full.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Snow Block]]|None|[[Snowball]] 4x Snowball|Recipe: Snowball ×4 → [[Snow Block]]|
### [[Snow Block]]
While not possible in a crafting table, with a [[Super Compactor 3000]], a [[Snow Minion]] will craft 10 stacks of Snowballs into an [[Enchanted Snow Block]].
## Trivia
- It became commonly accessible with the 0.7.5 update. However, somewhat unknown, it was actually obtainable before then by using a [[Biome Stick|Taiga Biome Stick]], then using a [[Weather Wand]] to change the weather to rain, which would result in the snow in the Taiga, and then harvest the snow.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Snowball.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Now obtainable by fishing in the [[Jerry Pond]] and [[Snow Minion]]|

|[[Sea Creature Ring]]|Sponge VI (6)|1x [[Sea Creature Talisman]] 2x [[Enchanted Sponge]] [[Sponge]] 6x Sponge|Recipe: Sponge ×6 + [[Enchanted Sponge]] ×2 + [[Sea Creature Talisman]] → [[Sea Creature Ring]]|
|[[Training Dummy]]|Pumpkin V (5) ([[Pumpkin]])|1x Armor Stand (Furniture) ([[Furniture]]) 1x [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] [[Sponge]] 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) Sponge|Recipe: Sponge x32 ×7 + [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] + [[Furniture]] → [[Training Dummy]]|
|[[Sponge Belt]]|Sponge V (5)|6x [[Enchanted Sponge]] [[Sponge]] 64x Sponge|Recipe: Sponge x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sponge]] x2 ×3 → [[Sponge Belt]]|
|[[Sponge Sinker]]|Sponge II (2)|[[Sponge]] 8x Sponge 16x [[Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Lily Pad]] x16 + Sponge x4 ×2 → [[Sponge Sinker]]|
## Trivia
- It takes approximately 50 days for a Tier 11 Fishing minion to complete this collection. Any tier below that takes approximately 100 days to complete this collection.
- Placing the sponge near water doesn't suck up nearby water, nor does it turn into a [[Wet Sponge]]. The only way to do so is by using a [[Plumber's Sponge]].

|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Sponge Collection Added.​|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed rarity from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to COMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed smelting [[Wet Sponge]] granting Sponge collection progress.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Added [[Sponge Belt]] to the collection.|

## Obtaining
Spruce Log is obtainable by breaking down Spruce Logs in the [[Spruce Woods]] in [[The Park]] and from [[Spruce Minion|Spruce Minions]]. Breaking Spruce Logs grants +6 [[Foraging]] XP. It is a [[Collections|Collectible]] under the [[Foraging]] section.

# Sticky Piston
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items
Sticky Piston is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Sticky Piston.
[Sticky Piston]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 12 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PISTON_STICKY_BASE
Tooltip: COMMON
A Sticky Piston is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be crafted and placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Piston]] 1x [[Slimeball]]|Recipe: [[Slimeball]] + [[Piston]] → Sticky Piston|
## Usage
### Private Island
Sticky Pistons can be placed as a decorative block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. However, like [[Piston|Pistons]], they cannot be activated from [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]].
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|Please note that pistons are currently disabled on this island!|Placing a Sticky Piston|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Sticky Piston.|
Linked from: [[Slimeball]], [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks]]

# Stone
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
Stone is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Stone.
[Stone]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STONE
Tooltip: COMMON BLOCK
Stone is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Stone can be mined with [[Silk Touch]] on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]], as well as on various public islands, including the [[Hub Island]], [[Gold Mine]], [[Deep Caverns]], [[Dwarven Mines]], and [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
In the [[Crystal Hollows]], stone blocks grants [[Hard Stone]] when mined. When [[Silk Touch]] is not used, [[Cobblestone]] is obtained instead.

### Shop Purchase
Stone can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 2 [[Coins]].
### Smelting
|Ingredients|Recipe|
|-|-|
|1x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] + [[Coal]] → Stone|
## Usage
### Placing
Stone can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. It can also be placed in the [[Crystal Hollows]] or in [[Glacite Mineshafts]].

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Stone]]|Recipe: [[Stone]] ×2 → Stone Pressure Plate|
### Shop Purchase
A Stone Pressure Plate can be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] for 4 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Stone Pressure Plates can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. Its functionality only work on the [[Private Island]], but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often it can be used. Redstone is disabled on [[The Garden]].

|-|-|-|-|

## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|[[Sugar Cane Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|250|[[Speed Talisman]] Recipe|+4|
|III|500|[[Enchanted Sugar]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Speedster Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,000|[[Speed Ring]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Enchanted Paper]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Magic For Pets In A Hurry]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|20,000|[[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|[[Speed Artifact]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Sugar Cane can be harvested at the [[Mushroom Desert]] or [[The Garden]].
Sugar Cane can be generated by [[Sugar Cane Minion|Sugar Cane Minions]].
Sugar Cane can be bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] for 10 [[Coins]] each.
Sugar Cane can be harvested from certain mutations in the [[Greenhouse]].

## Gallery
- Sugar Cane in the Greenhouse Sugar Cane in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Trivia
- Sugar cane farms can also be used to level up [[Alchemy]] very, very quickly, since each batch of [[Speed Potion|Speed VII Potions]] (created with [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] and [[Enchanted Glowstone]]) equates to about +54,000 [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] with just an [[Potions|Alchemy XP Boost Potion]] and nothing else.

|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased the NPC sell price of Sugar Cane from 2 [[Coins]] to 5 [[Coins]].|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Decreased the NPC sell price of Sugar Cane from 5 [[Coins]] to 4 [[Coins]].|

### Growth Stages of Sunflower in the Garden
- Stage 1
- Stage 2
- Stage 3
- Stage 4
## Tips
- In order to obtain the first Sunflower to plant on an [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] or [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profile, players must:
  - Get a [[Dragonfly]] [[Pests|Pest]], vacuum it up, and then paste the default Sunflower preset in the [[The Garden|Desk]] using the [[Enchanted Sunflower|Enchanted Sunflowers]]. After that, they can harvest the pasted crops at daytime to get regular Sunflowers. Harvesting them at nighttime will grant [[Moonflower|Moonflowers]] instead.
  - Players may also obtain it by growing a [[Godseed]] in the [[Greenhouse]]. However, this is not recommended because it takes a really long time. In addition, Greenhouse crop drops are automatically compacted, so it may still be necessary to paste the default preset using the drops.
## History

|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Made Sunflower a Garden ([[The Garden]]) crop.|

# Swords
Aliases: Sword, Thick Wooden Sword
Categories: Vanilla objects, Swords, Items, Combat, Weapons, Melee Weapons
[Swords]
A sword is a melee [[Weapons|weapon]] which has a "Sword" tag in its tooltip. They can be enhanced with [[Enchantments]] and [[Reforging|reforges]] for extra [[Stats]].
Some swords are subcategorized to different [[Sword Types]], which receive buffs from specific enchantments.

## Vanilla Swords
Show/Hide Vanilla Swords
Swords is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Swords.
All vanilla Minecraft swords exist in SkyBlock, although SkyBlock alternatives are almost always better. The wooden version is used in various [[Minions|Minion]] recipes.

## Calculate Max Damage
The damage dealt with the sword can be calculated below.
calcpage  = Calculator:Max damage
 template  = Template:Calculator/Max_damage
 form      = maxDmgCalcForm
 calcname  = Damage Calculator
 result    = inner

 param     = dmg|Damage|5|int|0-999999
 param     = str|Strength|0|int|0-999999
 param     = critdmg|Crit Damage|50|int|0-999999
 param     = multi|Multiplier|1|number|0-999999
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Sword Upgrades
### Enchantments
Multiple [[Enchantments|Sword Enchantments]] can be applied to Swords to give them additional stats and effects.
### Reforges
[[Reforging/Sword|Sword Reforges]] can be applied to Swords to give them bonus stats and effects, depending on the Reforge.
### Hot Potato Books
Up to 10 [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] can be applied to Swords in an [[Anvil]], giving the Sword a bonus +2 [[Damage]] and +2 [[Strength]] per Hot Potato Book.
### Fuming Potato Books
[[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]] function like Hot Potato Books, but can only be applied to a Sword 5 times. They can be applied even if the Sword already has Hot Potato Books applied.
### Art of War
An [[The Art of War|Art of War]] can be applied to a Sword to give it +5 [[Strength]].
### Recombobulator 3000
The [[Rarity]] of a Sword can be upgraded once by applying a [[Recombobulator 3000]] to it in an [[Anvil]].
### Gemstone Slots
[[Gemstone|Gemstones]] can be applied to a Sword's [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] to give it stat bonuses.
### Power Scrolls
A [[Power Scrolls|Power Scroll]] can be applied to a Sword with a RIGHT CLICK Ability to give it a special effect when its Ability is used.
### Dungeon Stars
Certain Swords can be upgraded with [[Star Upgrades]], which increase the stats of the Swords in [[Dungeons]]. Swords can be given Stars by upgrading them with [[Essence]], or applying [[Master Star|Master Stars]] to them.
## Trivia
- Several of the sword’s abilities are based on kits from another Hypixel minigame, [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]].
- The [[Fancy Sword]] was unique in that because the enchantments it was purchased with cannot be removed, it was possible to have impossible combinations, such as First Strike and Triple Strike, as well as Sharpness and Smite.
  - Nowadays, Sharpness and Smite (as well as Bane of Arthropods) can be applied together. However, the combination of First and Triple Strike is still exclusive to the old Fancy Sword.
- No swords in SkyBlock have the texture of a Netherite tool, due to the fact that SkyBlock was on version 1.8.9 until very recently.
- Before the Galatea Update, many Swords used the Texture of an Axe (Such as the [[Halberd of the Shredded]]). This was changed to make it clear that they cannot deal damage to Mobs that now have the [[Woodland]] Type.
  - The Weapons Affected by this change were the [[Halberd of the Shredded]], [[Daedalus Blade]], [[Raider Sword|Raider Axe]], [[Enrager]] (Replaced with Tormentor) and the [[Ragnarock|Ragnarok]].
- The Maximum Effective [[Attack Speed]] for Swords is 82 , due to the way Attack Speed performs Rounding when regarding them. This is different for Shortbows, where the Maximum Effective Attack Speed is 100 .
  - This also means [[Thermodynamic Armor]]'s Full Set Bonus has less value for Swords, since they wont benefit as much from the Increase in the Attack Speed Cap.[Needs Confirmation]
- The [[Aspect of the Jerry]] is the only item to have an [[Ultimate Enchantments|Ultimate Enchant]] dedicated to it.
  - This being [[Ultimate Jerry]], which increases its base [[Damage]] from 1000% up to 5000%. Despite it being the largest Damage Modifier in the game, the Aspect of the Jerry is still comically useless.

# TNT
Aliases: Tnt
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
TNT is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at TNT.
[TNT]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 15 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TNT
Tooltip: COMMON
TNT is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]] and blown up.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Sand]] 5x [[Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Gunpowder]] ×5 + [[Sand]] ×4 → TNT|
### Shop Purchase
TNT can be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] for 50 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
TNT can be placed as block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]], and then activated using [[Flint and Steel]] or a [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] signal to ignite it and blow it up.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minecart with TNT]]|None|[[TNT]] 1x TNT 1x [[Minecart]]|Recipe: TNT + [[Minecart]] → [[Minecart with TNT]]|
## Tips
- TNT is often used in conjunction with the [[Builder's Wand]] and [[Builder's Ruler]] to blow up large parts of the player's [[Private Island]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added TNT.|
Linked from: [[Bob-omb]], [[Gunpowder]], [[Mad Redstone Engineer]], [[Minecart]], [[Minecart with TNT]], [[Power Dragon]], [[Private Island]], [[Revenant Horror]], [[Sand]], [[The Garden]], [[Vanilla Objects]], [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks]]

# Torch
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Torch is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Torch.
[Torch]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Sell: 0.3 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Coal]] 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] + [[Stick]] → Torch x4|
|None|1x [[Charcoal]] 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: [[Charcoal]] + [[Stick]] → Torch x4|

64 Torches can be purchased for 32 [[Coins]] from [[Lumina]].
## Usage
### Placing
Torchs can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in the [[The Garden]], or in the [[Crystal Hollows]].

# Trapped Chest
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Storages
Trapped Chest is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Trapped Chest.
[Trapped Chest]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRAPPED_CHEST
Tooltip: COMMON
A Trapped Chest is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be used to store items, like a regular [[Chest]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Chest]] 1x [[Tripwire Hook]]|Recipe: [[Tripwire Hook]] + [[Chest]] → Trapped Chest|
## Usage
### Storage
A Trapped Chest It can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]], including next to [[Chest|Chests]]. Up to 500 chests can be placed on an island. They cannot be placed on [[The Garden]].
When opened, they will emit a weak [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] signal, but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often this can be done.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Trapped Chest.|
Linked from: [[Chest]], [[Tripwire Hook]], [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks]]

# Tripwire Hook
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Tripwire Hook is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Tripwire Hook.
[Tripwire Hook]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRIPWIRE_HOOK
Tooltip: COMMON
A Tripwire Hook is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] block that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Iron Ingot]] 1x [[Stick]] 1x [[Planks]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] + [[Stick]] + [[Planks]] → Tripwire Hook x2|
### Shop Purchase
A Tripwire Hook can be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] for 4 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Tripwire Hooks can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. When two are placed on a [[Private Island]], connected with [[String]], and activated by walking onto the string, it will activate adjacent [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] blocks, but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often this can be done. Redstone is disabled on [[The Garden]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trapped Chest]]|None|1x [[Chest]] [[Tripwire Hook]] 1x Tripwire Hook|Recipe: Tripwire Hook + [[Chest]] → [[Trapped Chest]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Tripwire Hook.|

### Shop Purchase
A Water Bottle can be purchased from the [[Alchemist]] for 6 [[Coins]].

### Brewing
A Water Bottle can be used to brew an [[Awkward Potion]] or [[Weakness Potion]].

## Obtaining
### Trades
A Water Bucket can be obtained in exchange for 12 [[Coins]] in the [[Trades]] menu after unlocking [[Tropical Fish|Tropical Fish II (2)]].
### Shop Purchase
A Water Bucket can be purchased for 12 [[Coins]] from the [[Builder]].
### Buckets
A Water Bucket can be obtained by filling up a [[Bucket]] from a water source.
### Starting Chest
A Water Bucket can be found in the starting [[Chest]] that appears on a new [[Private Island]].
## Usage
### Water
An Water Bucket can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]] to create a [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] source block. Doing this will turn the Water Bucket back into a [[Bucket]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pond Island]]|Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])|[[Water Bucket]] 3x Water Bucket 15x [[Sand]] 60x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: Water Bucket ×3 + [[Sand]] x5 ×3 + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x20 ×3 → [[Pond Island]]|
## Tips
- The [[Magical Water Bucket]] is a version of the Water Bucket that does not turn into a [[Bucket]] when used.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Water Bucket.|

- Wheat in the Greenhouse Wheat in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Tips
- Harvesting Wheat at the [[Farm]] is a good strategy early game for gaining [[Coins]] and leveling up the [[Farming]] Skill.

[[Experience]] Orbs: 2-19

A Witch is a hostile [[Mob]] which can only spawn on a player's [[Private Island]].

## Drops
Item drops - [Lv2-18]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gunpowder]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Glowstone Dust]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Glass Bottle]]|2|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|20%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv2-18]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1-6|1-6|1-6|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5-27|5-27|5-27|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|2-19|2-19|2-19|
## Behavior
Attacks from a range by throwing Splash debuff [[Potions]]. The player will be notified in chat when they are de-buffed from a Potion.
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Private Island ([[Bestiary/List]])|Witch|Masters of alchemy.|2|5|50|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|10|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|15|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|25|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|50|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Tips
- Witches spawn very rarely. A good way to farm them (e.g. for [[Bestiary]]) is to make a big platform to spawn mobs, and when it seems like no more mobs are spawning, leave the island for about 10 seconds (making sure there is no one else on it) and then return to it, and then wait 1-2 minutes for mobs to spawn again. If any Witches show up, kill them, and then repeat the process.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

### Crafting

### Private Island
Wooden Pressure Plates can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. Its functionality only work on the [[Private Island]], but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often it can be used. Redstone is disabled on [[The Garden]].

[Wool]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WOOL (White); WOOL:1 (Orange); WOOL:2 (Magenta); WOOL:3 (Light Blue); WOOL:4 (Yellow); WOOL:5 (Lime); WOOL:6 (Pink); WOOL:7 (Gray); WOOL:8 (Light Gray); WOOL:9 (Cyan); WOOL:10 (Purple); WOOL:11 (Blue); WOOL:12 (Brown); WOOL:13 (Green); WOOL:14 (Red); WOOL:15 (Black)
Tooltip: COMMON
Wool is a set of [[Rarity|COMMON]] Blocks that can be purchased from the [[Wool Weaver]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
White Wool drops from the following [[Mob|Mobs]]:
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Sheep]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Shears
[[Shears]] can be used on [[Sheep]] to collect [[Wool|White Wool]] from them. If this is used with the [[Rainbow]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]], a random color of wool will be dropped.
### Shop Purchase
All colors of Wool can be purchased from the [[Wool Weaver]] for 32 [[Coins]] each.

# Zombie
Aliases: Diamond Zombie, Enchanted Diamond Zombie, Zombies
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Zombie is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Zombie.
[Zombie]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1-15
Spawn Location: [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 100-707; [[Damage]]: 20-111
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Rotten Flesh]] 0–1x [[Potato]] 0–1x [[Carrot]] 0–1x [[Poisonous Potato]]
XP: 5-22 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1-16
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_#
The Zombie is a hostile [[Mob]] that spawns on the [[Private Island]].
## Spawning
Zombies can spawn naturally on the [[Private Island]] in dark areas. Placing a [[Repelling Candle]] on a Private Island can prevent Zombies from spawning.

## Behavior
Zombies spawn naturally on the [[Private Island|Player's Island]] in the dark. Zombies spawn at different levels and health depending upon the location they are found.

# Zombie Villager
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Zombie Villager is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Zombie Villager.
[Zombie Villager]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 2
Spawn Location: [[Graveyard]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 120; [[Damage]]: 24
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Rotten Flesh]] 0–1x [[Potato]] 0–1x [[Carrot]] 0–1x [[Poisonous Potato]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 2
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_VILLAGER_1
A Zombie Villager is a slightly stronger version of the [[Graveyard Zombie]] that spawns in the [[Graveyard]] at night (8pm-6am). They spawn wearing Leather Armor.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv2]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Rotten Flesh]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Poisonous Potato]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[[Potato]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[[Carrot]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv2]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|2|2|2|
## Strategy
Using an [[Intimidation Talisman]] from the [[Combat Merchant]], which costs 10,000 [[Coins]], will prevent Zombie Villagers from attacking. If the player can be easily killed by Zombie Villagers, a ranged weapon such as a [[Bows|Bow]] should be used to attack. Zombie Villagers are not able to be outrun with base [[Speed]] of 100, as they have 150 [[Speed]].
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Hub ([[Bestiary/List]])|Zombie Villager|The real enemy isn't the dead - it's the living.|1|5|200|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|40|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|60|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|100|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|200|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Trivia
- When interacted with the [[Bartender]], he will talk about Zombie Villagers.
- During The [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] event 2021, Zombie Villagers could drop [[Enchanted Poisonous Potato|Enchanted Poisonous Potatoes]] to obtain them faster in order to craft [[Poisoned Candy]].

# Game Annihilator
Categories: Very Special items, Items, Special items, Hypixel Gifts
[Game Annihilator]
Type: Item
Rarity: VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: This item was given to a player who reported enough bugs to make the SkyBlock developers cry.
Source: Hypixel Staff
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GAME_ANNIHILATOR
Tooltip: This item was given to a player who reported enough game breaking bugs to make the SkyBlock developers cry.; * Co-op Soulbound * VERY SPECIAL
Game Annihilator is a [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] item rewarded to players who've reported large amounts of game-breaking bugs in SkyBlock. It is related to the [[Game Breaker]] item, which is obtained from a single bug report.
## Obtaining
The Game Annihilator has only been obtained by four players for reporting 75+ game-breaking exploits in the span of a few months.
## Gallery
- The Game Annihilator on display
- The Game Annihilator as seen on four player's heads
## Trivia
- On the internal Hypixel testing network, a Game Annihilator [[Fire Sale]] was once hosted.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Feb 6, 2021|Added Game Annihilator.|
|Jul 4, 2023|Changed rarity from SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) to VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]).|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Game Annihilator is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|

# Hub Villagers
Aliases: Andrew, Duke, Felix, Jack, Jamie, Leo, Liam, Lynn, Ryu, Stella, Tom, Vex, Village Villagers, Villager, Villagers
Categories: Village, Locations, Mechanics, Quest giver
The Hub Villagers were [[NPC|NPCs]] that appeared in [[Hub Island|The Hub]] that were removed. However, they still appear as [[The Garden|Visitors]] on [[The Garden]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Andrew in [[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Andrew: Did you know that SkyBlock has been running for [number] in-game months already? Time flies.|
|Andrew in [[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Andrew: See you around!|
|Andrew in [[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Andrew: I guess I'll come back in a bit.|
|Jack in [[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Jack: Did you know that you gain additional ☘ Farming Fortune while on the The Garden? You can check it in your SkyBlock Profile!|
|Jack in [[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Jack: Thanks for the crops. See ya around.|
|Jack in [[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Jack: I'll come by later.|
|Jamie in [[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Jamie: You might have noticed that you can purchase additional plots within your Desk in the Barn!|

# Map (Hub)
Aliases: Hub Map, The Map
Categories: Village
The Map, also called Hub Map, is a location on the [[Hub Island]] that shows all [[Locations|Public Islands]]. It is located on the front wall of the [[Community Center]].
## Map Revisions
|Map Revision On|Description|
|-|-|
|New Prototype Game - Hypixel SkyBlock|The first map of Public Islands. Added [[Hub Island]], [[Floating Islands]], [[Spider's Den]], [[Blazing Fortress]], [[Gold Mine]], [[Deep Caverns]], [[The Barn]], and [[Mushroom Desert]].|
|2020/February 28|The second map of Public Islands. Replaced [[Floating Islands]] with [[The Park]], and added [[The End]].|
|Revamped Hub!|The third map of Public Islands with major changes to the [[Hub Island]].|
|April 7, 2021|The Hub Map temporarily replaced by a message from [[Dante]].|
|April 16, 2021|The Hub Map temporarily replaced by a message from Seraphine ([[Clerk Seraphine]]).|
|Farming Island Revamp|The fourth map of Public Islands with major changes to the [[Mushroom Desert]] and the [[Spider's Den]].|
|April 1, 2022; yearly thereafter|The Hub Map was temporarily replaced on [[April Fools]] day.|
|SkyBlock Patch 0.13 - Crimson Isle|The fifth map of Public Islands. Replaced [[Blazing Fortress]] with [[Crimson Isle]].|
|Hypixel SkyBlock 0.23 - The Foraging Update|The sixth map of Public Islands. Revamped [[The Park]] and added [[Galatea]].|
|Hypixel SkyBlock 0.24.2 - Hub Revamp, Latest Minecraft, Year of the Witch, and more!|The seventh map of Public Islands. Revamped alongside the Hub Island revamp.|
## Trivia
- The Map consists of a 13x7 grid of 91 small Minecraft Maps in item frames. These item frames are placed on [[Sea Lantern|Sea Lanterns]] which keep the map grid bright and easy to see.
- For the second, third and fourth maps, [[Launch Pad (Structure)|Launch Pads]] are marked on the map with semi-visible thin lines. This was changed when the 5th map established clear logos for Slime Launcher locations and directions.
- The second and third maps featured text above the islands indicating the location names.
- Historically, the Map is only replaced when major appearance changes happen on any public island.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added the Hub World Map|
|Feb 28, 2020|Updated the Hub World Map to show new locations|
|Aug 13, 2020|0.8.1|Updated the Hub World Map to be consistent with the reconstruction of the [[Village]]|
|Aug 13, 2020|0.8.1|The Hub Map was moved from near Duke ([[Hub Villagers]]) and Lynn ([[Hub Villagers]]) in the village, to the front wall of the [[Community Center]].|
|Apr 7, 2021|The Hub World Map was replaced by a message from [[Dante]]|
|Apr 16, 2021|The Hub World Map was replaced by a message from Seraphine ([[Clerk Seraphine]])|
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Updated the map to show The Farming Islands ([[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)]]) update|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Updated the map to show the Nether Update, replacing [[Blazing Fortress]] with [[Crimson Isle]]|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Updated the map to show the Foraging Update, revamping [[The Park]] and adding [[Galatea]].|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Revamped the map with the release of the 1.21 Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|

# Wands
Aliases: Healing Wand, Healing Wands, Healing wand
Categories: Wands
Wands are a class of items with a magic ability.
## Mechanics
A Wand is any item which is tagged as a Wand item in its description, such as the [[Wand of Atonement]]. Not all items with "Wand" in their name are considered Wands; some may instead be [[Swords]] (eg. [[Ice Spray Wand]]) or have no designation (eg. [[Builder's Wand]]).
Wands are items with magic abilities and can have [[Ultimate Wise]] applied to them.

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|

## Usage
Sword:
Zombie Commander Whip [[Damage]]: +65 [[Strength]]: +5
[Effects]
[Ability: Commander Whip; Every 1 Zombies killed during a dungeon run by this weapon gives the wielder +1 Strength.]
The Zombie Commander Whip can be used for melee damage, like any sword, but can also be cast like a fishing rod, dealing your standard melee damage to all enemies that are hit. While in the [[Catacombs]], it also gains +1 [[Strength]] for every 1 zombie killed.
### Stats Scaling
The base [[Stats]] of this Item varies based on the floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] it was obtained on. The stats can also be increased by a random amount, up to 50%.

# Blood God Crest
Categories: Year of the Pig, Common items, Accessories, Crafting ingredients
Year of the Pig Exclusive Blood God Crest is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Blood God Crest]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Blood God Sigil]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLOOD_GOD_CREST
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Tracks a counter for each mob killed while worn.; Gain +1 Strength for each digit on the counter. (Max 7); Counter: None yet!; Right-click to view recipes!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Blood God Crest is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that is rarely dropped by [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]].
## Obtaining
### Shiny Pigs
A Blood God Crest has a 0.1% (1 in 1035.5) chance to be dropped by [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]]. The chance can be increased to 0.14% (1 in 690.83 pigs) with [[Pig Brain Mixin]] active.
In addition, after completing 1,000 [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]], next drop is guaranteed to be the Blood God Crest. Dropping 2 items from a [[Shiny Pig]] will count double towards pity. The player's progress towards this can be viewed using the [[Pity and RNG Meters|/pity]] command.
### Stranded
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], multiple Blood God Crests can be purchased from [[Stranded|Villagers]] for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Emerald|Emeralds]] each.
## Usage
Accessory:
Blood God Crest
[Effects]
[Tracks a counter for each Mob killed while worn.; Gain +1 Strength for each digit on the counter.]
The Blood God Crest tracks how many mobs its user has killed while equipped. It grants +1 [[Strength]] for each digit in its kill counter, capped at +7 [[Strength]]. For example, 1–9 kill(s) grants +1 [[Strength]], 10–99 kills grants +2 [[Strength]], and so on. This is a logarithmic relation, since each stat increase requires ten times as many kills. The amount of [[Strength]] that granted matches this formula: Strength=⌊log10(Number of Kills)⌋+1
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blood God Sigil]]|None|[[Blood God Crest]] 1x Blood God Crest 32x [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] 64x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x16 ×4 + Blood God Crest → [[Blood God Sigil]]|
## Trivia
- This item was added as part of [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]'s mayor perks, due to his position as the [[Resistance Leader]].
  - It could be purchased from the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and 32 [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]].
- This item seems to be the [[Resistance Movement|Resistance]] counterpart of the [[Dante's Talisman]].
- This item's texture is a miniaturized version of [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]'s skin, mainly evident by the torso region and crown.
- This item was made buyable again in 2022 after [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]'s unfortunate death, along with the rest of the [[Piggy|Technoshop]], this also led to it being added back multiple times.
- This item was the one of the most expensive [[Accessories]] that could be purchased with [[Coins]] from [[NPC|NPCs]] for 500,000–1,000,000 [[Coins]], behind the [[Dante's Talisman]] and [[Dante's Ring]] which are sold for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] each.

# Dante's Ring
Aliases: Dante Ring
Categories: Year of the Pig, Common items, Accessories, Ring
Year of the Pig Exclusive Dante's Ring is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Dante's Ring]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])

Buy: 1,000,000 [[Coins]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Shiny Token]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DANTE_RING
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Found in a hidden warehouse. Not very useful but still pretty nice to wear.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Dante's Ring is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It serves as an alternative to [[Dante's Talisman]], and only one of the two will award [[Accessory Power]] while in the [[Accessory Bag]].
## Obtaining
Dante's Ring can be purchased from [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and 128 (2 stacks) [[Shiny Token]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
Dante's Ring and [[Dante's Talisman]] share an [[Accessory Power]] slot, so equipping both does not grant extra [[Accessory Power]]. Players who own neither can use whichever one they can obtain.
## Trivia
- Before the [[Year of the Pig]] event was introduced, Dante's Ring was obtainable from the [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] during the [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] Event.
- Dante's Ring was added during the 4th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] as a replacement for [[Dante's Talisman]], which could no longer be obtained.
## History

# Flying Pig
Categories: Year of the Pig, Rare items, Items, Pet items
Year of the Pig Exclusive Flying Pig is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Flying Pig]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_FLYING_PIG
Tooltip: Consumed on use; This pet item may only be used on a Pig pet.; Grants +40 Potato Fortune and +20 Speed.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; RARE PET ITEM
Flying Pig is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] obtained from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]]. It grants +40 [[Crop Fortune|Potato Fortune]] and +20 [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Shiny Pigs
Flying Pig has a 0.14% (1 in 690.33) chance to be dropped by [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pig Pet]] whilst holding a Flying Pig will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +40 [[Crop Fortune|Potato Fortune]] and +20 [[Speed]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Flying Pig for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- The name of this item may be a reference to the popular saying "when pigs fly".
- The Flying Pig's stats are a reference to 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade: the [[Crop Fortune|Potato Fortune]] referencing [[The Great Potato War]] and the [[Speed]] referencing Technoblade's goal to gain the most [[Speed]] possible.
- Prior to version 0.24.2, the Flying Pig allowed the player to fly in the [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]] while riding the pet, as the [[Pig Pet]] used to be the actual mob.
- Flying Pig was first obtainable from the [[Ancient Pig]], then from [[Raffle of the Century]] event during Year 300 and Year 400, before being added as a [[Year of the Pig]] drop.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 18, 2026|0.24.2 RC|Flying Pig now gives +40 Potato Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]) and +20 [[Speed]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|May 27, 2021|Added Flying Pig.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Flying Pig is now obtainable from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]).|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Flying Pig now gives +40 Potato Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]) and +20 [[Speed]].|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added NPC sell price of 500,000 [[Coins]].|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|100 [[Coins]]|1x [[Small Pig's Ear]]|1 Minute|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|1x [[Medium Pig's Ear]]|1 Hour|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|1x [[Large Pig's Ear]]|1 Day|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|1x [[Giant Pig's Ear]]|3 Days|
## Trivia
- During the first few hours after release, a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Pig Pet could trample crops on other players' islands.
- The Pig Pet used to be a [[Farming]] Mount.

- A summoned Pig Pet prior to 0.24.2

# Piggy
Aliases: Pig Shop, Technoshop
Categories: Year of the Pig, Shops, Non-player characters, The Uprising, Special items
Year of the Pig Exclusive Piggy is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Piggy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells items during the [[Year of the Pig]] event.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 15.5 71 -8.5
Piggy (also known as the Pig Shop and previously called the Technoshop) is a shop where players can buy certain exclusive items during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Interface
- [[Piggy/UI|Year of the Pig]]
- [[Piggy/UI 1|The Uprising]]
- [[Piggy/UI 2|3rd Anniversary]]
- [[Piggy/UI 3|4th Anniversary]]
- [[Piggy/UI 3|5th Anniversary]]
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Piggy: Oink oink! Pig time! Year of the Pig! It's here, oink oink! [NPC] Piggy: You take Shiny Orb, plop it on pig - oink! [NPC] Piggy: Then you chase piggy, chase piggy! [NPC] Piggy: Guide piggy back to orb, and poof! [NPC] Piggy: Piggy leave shiny treasure for you. [NPC] Piggy: Get shiny stuff, keep shiny stuff! Easy, oink! [NPC] Piggy: Now go, go! Piggy no wait forever!|
## Gallery
- Piggy in their area near the Museum
## Trivia
- In the game's lore, the coins spent in the first edition of the Technoshop were used to fix the [[Hub Island]] in the wake of the [[Dante|battle]] against [[Dante]].
- The [[Shiny Pig]] mechanic, as well as [[The Art of War]] and [[Dante's Ring]] were added into the game as a result of the [[Resistance Movement]], inspired by a player become mayor, [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]].
- The cost to buy 1 of every item in Piggy's shop except for [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]] is 1,056 (16 stacks plus 32) [[Shiny Token]], 6,000,000 [[Coins]], 2 [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]], and 144 (2 stacks plus 16) [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]].
