# Potato Talisman
Categories: Year of the Pig, Common items, Accessories, Crafting ingredients, Talisman, Great Potato War, Special items
Year of the Pig Exclusive Potato Talisman is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Potato Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]); [[Potato King]] (unavailable)
Stats: [[Speed]]: 1
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Potato Ring]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POTATO_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Speed: +1; Accessory Power: +3; Your potato minions farm 5% faster while wearing on your island.; Forged by the Potato King after winning the Great Potato War.; Right-click to view recipes!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Potato Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] made to commemorate the Great Potato War.
## Obtaining
The Potato Talisman has a 0.05% (1 in 2071 pigs) to drop from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]]. The chance is increased to 0.07% (1 in 1381.66 pigs) with [[Pig Brain Mixin]] active.
In addition, after completing 2,000 [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]], next drop is guaranteed to be the Potato Talisman. Dropping 2 items from a [[Shiny Pig]] will count double towards pity. The player's progress towards this can be viewed using the [[Pity and RNG Meters|/pity]] command.
Every player was able to claim a Potato Talisman from the [[Potato King]] near the [[Map (Hub)|Map]] from June 21, 2020 - June 26, 2020. It could also be a rare drop from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] events, with a drop chance of 0.02%.
## Usage
The Potato Talisman grants +1 [[Speed]]. If the player has it in their inventory or [[Accessory Bag]], and is on their private island, it increases the speed of all [[Potato Minion|Potato Minions]] on the island, by 5%.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Potato Ring]]|None|[[Potato Talisman]] 1x Potato Talisman 64x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x8 ×8 + Potato Talisman → [[Potato Ring]]|
## Trivia
- This item heavily references "Technoblade", a Minecraft YouTuber who was the first player to reach 500 million Potatoes in their collection.
  - Technoblade's journey to 500 million was a hard-won fight against player-turned-YouTuber "im_a_squid_kid". This became an event in the SkyBlock YouTube community, known as the "Great Potato War," which lasted over nine months. im_a_squid_kid's count at the time of Technoblade's surpassing him was around 412 million.
    - Simon (Hypixel's founder) himself even chimed in on Twitter, originally suggesting the war end at one billion (1,000,000,000) potatoes farmed. This was used as a catalyst for Technoblade and im_a_squid_kid to agree on a final count, settling at the 500 million mark.
  - The [[Potato King]], who granted this talisman its skin, was Technoblade. It is his skin, with a [[Potato]] on his head.
  - The +1 [[Speed]] may be in reference to the [[Pig's Foot]], which was added before the Great Potato War when Technoblade won a tournament called "Minecraft Monday," which was given its stats thanks to Technoblade's focus on [[Speed]] in his early SkyBlock videos.
- Like the [[Crab Hat of Celebration]], [[Sloth Hat of Celebration]], and every unique [[New Year Cake]], the Potato Talisman could only be claimed once per profile.
- Immediately after this update, the Potato Talisman could be placed on Private Islands. This was swiftly patched, although some players who had already placed the talisman did not have the blocks removed, so some instances may still remain.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Shiny Orb
Categories: Year of the Pig, Common items, Items, The Uprising, Special items
Year of the Pig Exclusive Shiny Orb is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[Shiny Orb]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No

[Shop]

Buy: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHINY_ORB
Tooltip: Right-click shiny pigs with this item.; Bring the pig back to the orb to gain loot.; COMMON
Shiny Orbs are [[Rarity|COMMON]] items related to the [[Year of the Pig]] event. They can be used on a [[Shiny Pig]] to obtain rewards.
## Obtaining
During the [[Year of the Pig]] event, Shiny Orbs can be purchased from the [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] for 5,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Shiny Pig]] with a Shiny Orb will launch the pig a short distance away while the orb hovers in place where it was clicked. Bringing the pig back to the orb within 90 seconds will grant a reward.
## Loot
Upon bringing a [[Shiny Pig]] back to a [[Shiny Orb]] Shiny Orb, the player receives a [[Shiny Token]] and one reward from the table below.
- The [[Pig Brain Mixin]] grants a +10% chance to loot a 2nd item from the loot table below.
- The amount of [[Coins]] can be increased by the following sources, stacking additively:
[[Coins]] Modifiers on [[Shiny Orb]] Shiny Orbs:

|+25%||[[Shiny Rod]]|
|+30%|[[Bestiary]]|Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])|
|+0.20% → 20%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|+0.35% → 35%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|+0.50% → 50%|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Pig Pet]]|
|Total = +105%|||

## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|On Interaction with a Shiny Pig of another player|Oink! This pig has already been clicked by someone else!|
|On Bringing a Shiny Pig to its Orb|SHINY! The orb is charged! Click on it for loot!|
|On Clicking on a Shiny Orb charged by the player|SHINY! You extracted [#]x [item] from the pigglet's orb!|
|On Clicking on a Shiny Orb charged by another player|Only the orb's owner may click on it!|
|When Shiny Pig is not brought to its Orb after 90 seconds|Your Shiny Orb and associated pig expired and disappeared.|
|Condition|Message|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig without holding a Shiny Orb|Oink! Use a Shiny Orb from the Pig Shop to capture the shiny from this piggy!|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig when holding a Shiny Orb when two other Shiny Pigs are active|You may only "own" up to 2 shiny pigs at once.|
|On Interaction with Shiny Pig when holding a Shiny Orb otherwise|Oink! Bring the pig back to the Shiny Orb!|
## Trivia
- When the [[Blood God Shrine]] was active, Shiny Orbs had a very small chance to give the player a [[Shiny Relic]].
  - This feature was removed when the [[Ancient Pig]] was summoned.
- Shiny Orbs were added as part of 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade's mayor perks, due to his position as the [[Resistance Leader]].
- Although the 'Shiny Orb's description says to right-click a [[Shiny Pig]], left-clicking them also works.
- The message received in the chat after clicking a charged orb has a misspelling. The word "piglet's" is spelled with two g's in the message.
- Shiny Orbs could previously be placed on [[Private Island|Private Islands]]. This got patched, but some islands still have remaining orbs.
- In the first days of the event, about 9 million [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] were killed every 24 hours.
- During the 21 days that the original event lasted, 53 million Shiny Orbs were used.

A Small Pig's Ear is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used to upgrade a [[Pig Pet]] to [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] at [[Kat]].
## Obtaining
A Small Pig's Ear can be purchased from [[Piggy]] for 32 [[Shiny Token]] and 64 (1 stack) [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
A Small Pig's Ear can be used to upgrade a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Pig Pet]] to [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]].
